#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
Each Floyd–Warshall step depends on the previous pivot, so pivot order must stay sequential. I’ll use a persistent OpenMP team to divide rows and vectorize the inner loop, preserving both distance and path updates.

/bin/bash -lc 'cat floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r5
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {

+    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
+    #pragma omp parallel

+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+
+    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
+    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

/bin/bash -lc 'cat floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r5
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* const row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();

    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

            print(n,threads,re.search(r'Computation time: (\\d+)',result)[1],flush=True)

+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

codex
The first version matches the original results at every tested size and scales from 2.2 seconds with one thread to 92 milliseconds with 32 threads for 2,048 nodes. I’m checking whether safe bounds can skip row updates once the shortest paths converge.

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowMaximum(numNodes);
+    unsigned int minimumEdge = INF;
+
+    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
+    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minimumEdge)
         for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+            const unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+            unsigned int maximum = 0;
+            unsigned int minimum = INF;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum) reduction(min:minimum)
             for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+                maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
+                if (j != i) {
+                    minimum = std::min(minimum, row[j]);
+                }
+            }
+            rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
+            minimumEdge = std::min(minimumEdge, minimum);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                // Every off-diagonal distance is at least minimumEdge. The
+                // j == k candidate equals row[k], so cannot improve either.
+                if (distIK + minimumEdge >= rowMaximum[i]) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int maximum = 0;
+                #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum)
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
+                    maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
                 }
+                rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
             }
         }
     }

+            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
 }
 
 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowMaximum(numNodes);
+    unsigned int minimumEdge = INF;
+
+    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
+    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minimumEdge)
         for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
+            const unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+            unsigned int maximum = 0;
+            unsigned int minimum = INF;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum) reduction(min:minimum)
             for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+                maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
+                if (j != i) {
+                    minimum = std::min(minimum, row[j]);
+                }
+            }
+            rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
+            minimumEdge = std::min(minimumEdge, minimum);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                // Every off-diagonal distance is at least minimumEdge. The
+                // j == k candidate equals row[k], so cannot improve either.
+                if (distIK + minimumEdge >= rowMaximum[i]) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int maximum = 0;
+                #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum)
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < oldDist) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
+                    maximum = std::max(maximum, std::min(oldDist, newDist));
                 }
+                rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
             }
         }
     }

import os, re, subprocess
binary='/tmp/floydwarshall-openmp-build/floydwarshall'
for n in (128,512,1024,2048):
    for threads in (32,64,128,256,None):
        env=dict(os.environ)

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* const row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
    std::vector<unsigned int> rowMaximum(numNodes);
    unsigned int minimumEdge = INF;

    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minimumEdge)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            const unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
            unsigned int maximum = 0;
            unsigned int minimum = INF;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum) reduction(min:minimum)
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
                if (j != i) {
                    minimum = std::min(minimum, row[j]);
                }
            }
            rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
            minimumEdge = std::min(minimumEdge, minimum);
        }

        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                // Refresh an upper bound periodically rather than reducing
                // every update; distances only decrease between refreshes.
                if ((k & 31) == 0) {
                    unsigned int maximum = 0;
                    #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum)
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                        maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
                    }
                    rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
                }
                // Every off-diagonal distance is at least minimumEdge. The
                // j == k candidate equals row[k], so cannot improve either.
                if (distIK + minimumEdge >= rowMaximum[i]) {
                    continue;
                }
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < oldDist) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* const row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
    std::vector<unsigned int> rowMaximum(numNodes);
    unsigned int minimumEdge = INF;

    // Keep one team alive across all pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minimumEdge)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            const unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
            unsigned int maximum = 0;
            unsigned int minimum = INF;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum) reduction(min:minimum)
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
                if (j != i) {
                    minimum = std::min(minimum, row[j]);
                }
            }
            rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
            minimumEdge = std::min(minimumEdge, minimum);
        }

        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With a zero diagonal and nonnegative weights the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only avoids shared writes.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                // Refresh an upper bound periodically rather than reducing
                // every update; distances only decrease between refreshes.
                if ((k & 31) == 0) {
                    unsigned int maximum = 0;
                    #pragma omp simd reduction(max:maximum)
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                        maximum = std::max(maximum, row[j]);
                    }
                    rowMaximum[i] = maximum;
                }
                // Every off-diagonal distance is at least minimumEdge. The
                // j == k candidate equals row[k], so cannot improve either.
                if (distIK + minimumEdge >= rowMaximum[i]) {
                    continue;
                }
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < oldDist) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {

        } else {

            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);

    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

 void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
-    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {

+    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
+    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    constexpr size_t pivotBatch = 16;
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(std::min(pivotBatch, numNodes) * numNodes);
+    const int threads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+        static_cast<int>(std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / 16)));
+
+    // A persistent team reuses each row across a batch of ascending pivots.
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
+            const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
+            // Snapshot each pivot at its exact point in the serial algorithm.
+            // Earlier snapshots supply all dependencies within this batch.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                    std::memcpy(pivot, distances + k * numNodes,
+                                numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
+                    for (size_t p = first; p < k; ++p) {
+                        const unsigned int* const previous =
+                            pivotRows.data() + (p - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distKP = pivot[p];
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                            pivot[j] = std::min(pivot[j], distKP + previous[j]);
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
+
+            // Each row follows the original k order, including strict path
+            // updates on ties. Snapshots stay read-only until all rows finish.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    if (i == k) {
+                        continue; // A zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve.
+                    }
+                    const unsigned int* const pivot =
+                        pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                    const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                    const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                            row[j] = newDist;
+                            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

codex
Batching pivot snapshots lets each thread process several pivots per row before synchronizing. This preserves ascending pivot order and exact path choices, and reduces barriers from one per pivot to two per batch. The batched version also passes all 480 equivalence cases; I’m finishing performance tuning and build checks.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* const row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    constexpr size_t pivotBatch = 16;
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(std::min(pivotBatch, numNodes) * numNodes);
    const int threads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
        static_cast<int>(std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / 16)));

    // A persistent team reuses each row across a batch of ascending pivots.
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
    {
        for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
            const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
            // Snapshot each pivot at its exact point in the serial algorithm.
            // Earlier snapshots supply all dependencies within this batch.
            #pragma omp single
            {
                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
                    std::memcpy(pivot, distances + k * numNodes,
                                numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
                    for (size_t p = first; p < k; ++p) {
                        const unsigned int* const previous =
                            pivotRows.data() + (p - first) * numNodes;
                        const unsigned int distKP = pivot[p];
                        #pragma omp simd
                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                            pivot[j] = std::min(pivot[j], distKP + previous[j]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Each row follows the original k order and tie choices. Snapshots
            // stay read-only until all rows finish.
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                    if (i == k) {
                        continue; // A zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve.
                    }
                    const unsigned int* const pivot =
                        pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
                    const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                    const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                    #pragma omp simd
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                        const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
                        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                        row[j] = std::min(oldDist, newDist);
                        if (newDist < oldDist) {
                            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                            pivot[j] = std::min(pivot[j], distKP + previous[j]);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // Each row follows the original k order and tie choices. Snapshots
+            // stay read-only until all rows finish.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                std::array<unsigned int, pivotBatch> coefficients;
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    coefficients[k - first] = row[k];
+                }
+                // Capture row[k] at each pivot before changing column order.
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    const unsigned int* const pivot =
+                        pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                    const unsigned int distIK = coefficients[k - first];
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t p = k + 1; p < last; ++p) {
+                        coefficients[p - first] = std::min(coefficients[p - first],
+                                                          distIK + pivot[p]);
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes; column += columnTile) {
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile, numNodes);
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        if (i == k) {
+                            continue; // A zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve.
+                        }
+                        const unsigned int* const pivot =
+                            pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = coefficients[k - first];
+                        const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = column; j < columnEnd; ++j) {
+                            const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
+                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                            row[j] = std::min(oldDist, newDist);
+                            if (newDist < oldDist) {
+                                pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

    17	// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
    18	inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    19	    return j * n + i;
    20	}
    21	
    22	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
    23	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    24	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    25	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    26	    
    27	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
    28	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    29	    }
    30	    
    31	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    32	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    33	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    38	    // Both assignments in the original loop initialize a cell to its row.
    39	    // Write each cell once, in contiguous rows owned by separate threads.
    40	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    41	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    42	        unsigned int* const row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
    43	        #pragma omp simd
    44	        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    45	            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	}
    49	
    50	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
    51	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
    52	                   const size_t numNodes) {
    53	    constexpr size_t pivotBatch = 16;
    54	    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
    55	    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    56	    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
    57	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(std::min(pivotBatch, numNodes) * numNodes);
    58	    const int threads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
    59	        static_cast<int>(std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / 16)));
    60	
    61	    // A persistent team reuses each row across a batch of ascending pivots.
    62	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
    63	    {
    64	        for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
    65	            const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
    66	            // Snapshot each pivot at its exact point in the serial algorithm.
    67	            // Earlier snapshots supply all dependencies within this batch.
    68	            #pragma omp single
    69	            {
    70	                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
    71	                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
    72	                    std::memcpy(pivot, distances + k * numNodes,
    73	                                numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));
    74	                    for (size_t p = first; p < k; ++p) {
    75	                        const unsigned int* const previous =
    76	                            pivotRows.data() + (p - first) * numNodes;
    77	                        const unsigned int distKP = pivot[p];
    78	                        #pragma omp simd
    79	                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    80	                            pivot[j] = std::min(pivot[j], distKP + previous[j]);
    81	                        }
    82	                    }
    83	                }
    84	            }
    85	
    86	            // Each row follows the original k order and tie choices. Snapshots
    87	            // stay read-only until all rows finish.
    88	            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
    89	            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    90	                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
    91	                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
    92	                std::array<unsigned int, pivotBatch> coefficients;
    93	                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
    94	                    coefficients[k - first] = row[k];
    95	                }
    96	                // Capture row[k] at each pivot before changing column order.
    97	                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
    98	                    const unsigned int* const pivot =
    99	                        pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
   100	                    const unsigned int distIK = coefficients[k - first];
   101	                    #pragma omp simd
   102	                    for (size_t p = k + 1; p < last; ++p) {
   103	                        coefficients[p - first] = std::min(coefficients[p - first],
   104	                                                          distIK + pivot[p]);
   105	                    }
   106	                }
   107	                for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes; column += columnTile) {
   108	                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile, numNodes);
   109	                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
   110	                        if (i == k) {
   111	                            continue; // A zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve.
   112	                        }
   113	                        const unsigned int* const pivot =
   114	                            pivotRows.data() + (k - first) * numNodes;
   115	                        const unsigned int distIK = coefficients[k - first];
   116	                        const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
   117	                        #pragma omp simd
   118	                        for (size_t j = column; j < columnEnd; ++j) {
   119	                            const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
   120	                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
   121	                            row[j] = std::min(oldDist, newDist);
   122	                            if (newDist < oldDist) {
   123	                                pathRow[j] = intermediate;
   124	                            }
   125	                        }
   126	                    }
   127	                }
   128	            }
   129	        }
   130	    }
   131	}

   135
